The DENTEX dataset was preprocessed using Roboflow for format conversion and augmentation, yielding 1,082 images for tooth enumeration and 1,040 images for disease segmentation across four pathology classes. Five YOLOv26-seg variants were trained on Google Colab using transfer learning at a resolution of 800x800. Results demonstrate that the YOLOv26m-seg model achieved the best performance for tooth enumeration, with a precision of 0.976, recall of 0.970, and box mAP50 of 0.976. It outperformed the YOLOv8x baseline by 4.9% in precision and 3.3% in mAP50, while also enabling high-quality mask-level segmentation (mask mAP50 = 0.970). For disease segmentation, the YOLOv26l-seg model attained a box mAP50 of 0.591 and a mask mAP50 of 0.547. Impacted teeth showed the highest per-class average precision (0.943), indicating that visual distinctiveness influences detection performance more than annotation quantity. Overall, these findings demonstrate that YOLOv26-based models offer a robust and accurate framework for automated dental image analysis, with strong potential to enhance diagnostic efficiency and consistency in clinical practice.

</details>


### [75] [A Two-Stage, Object-Centric Deep Learning Framework for Robust Exam Cheating Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16234)
*Van-Truong Le,Le-Khanh Nguyen,Trong-Doanh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于YOLOv8n和RexNet-150的两阶段考试作弊检测框架，在27万样本数据集上达到95%准确率，比基线提升13%，推理时间13.9ms，兼顾技术性能与伦理隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工监考效率低、成本高且易出错；现有AI监控系统缺乏透明度或需要复杂架构。需要开发高效、透明、可扩展的考试作弊检测解决方案。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：1) YOLOv8n模型定位考场图像中的学生区域；2) 裁剪预处理后的区域由微调的RexNet-150模型分类为正常或作弊行为。使用10个独立来源共273,897个样本的数据集进行训练。

Result: 系统达到0.95准确率、0.94召回率、0.96精确率和0.95 F1分数，比视频作弊检测基线准确率0.82提升13%。平均推理时间13.9ms/样本，适合大规模部署。

Conclusion: 该研究为开发实时、可扩展、符合伦理且开源解决方案奠定了基础。系统通过私密方式（如个人邮箱）向学生反馈结果，避免公开羞辱。未来可整合音频数据和连续帧进一步提升准确性。

Abstract: Academic integrity continues to face the persistent challenge of examination cheating. Traditional invigilation relies on human observation, which is inefficient, costly, and prone to errors at scale. Although some existing AI-powered monitoring systems have been deployed and trusted, many lack transparency or require multi-layered architectures to achieve the desired performance. To overcome these challenges, we propose an improvement over a simple two-stage framework for exam cheating detection that integrates object detection and behavioral analysis using well-known technologies. First, the state-of-the-art YOLOv8n model is used to localize students in exam-room images. Each detected region is cropped and preprocessed, then classified by a fine-tuned RexNet-150 model as either normal or cheating behavior. The system is trained on a dataset compiled from 10 independent sources with a total of 273,897 samples, achieving 0.95 accuracy, 0.94 recall, 0.96 precision, and 0.95 F1-score - a 13\% increase over a baseline accuracy of 0.82 in video-based cheating detection. In addition, with an average inference time of 13.9 ms per sample, the proposed approach demonstrates robustness and scalability for deployment in large-scale environments. Beyond the technical contribution, the AI-assisted monitoring system also addresses ethical concerns by ensuring that final outcomes are delivered privately to individual students after the examination, for example, via personal email. This prevents public exposure or shaming and offers students an opportunity to reflect on their behavior. For further improvement, it is possible to incorporate additional factors, such as audio data and consecutive frames, to achieve greater accuracy. This study provides a foundation for developing real-time, scalable, ethical, and open-source solutions.

</details>


### [76] [CollideNet: Hierarchical Multi-scale Video Representation Learning with Disentanglement for Time-To-Collision Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16240)
*Nishq Poorav Desai,Ali Etemad,Michael Greenspan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CollideNet：一种用于碰撞时间预测的新型时空分层Transformer架构，通过多分辨率空间聚合和时序分量解耦实现SOTA性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞时间预测需要精确的时间预测和理解视频中的局部与全局时空模式，现有方法难以有效处理视频的多尺度特性

Method: 提出CollideNet，包含空间流（多分辨率同时聚合每帧信息）和时间流（多尺度特征编码，解耦非平稳性、趋势和季节性分量）

Result: 在三个常用公共数据集上达到SOTA性能，显著超越先前工作；通过跨数据集评估验证泛化能力，可视化分量解耦效果

Conclusion: CollideNet通过时空分层Transformer架构有效处理视频多尺度特性，在碰撞时间预测任务上取得突破性进展，具备良好泛化能力

Abstract: Time-to-Collision (TTC) forecasting is a critical task in collision prevention, requiring precise temporal prediction and comprehending both local and global patterns encapsulated in a video, both spatially and temporally. To address the multi-scale nature of video, we introduce a novel spatiotemporal hierarchical transformer-based architecture called CollideNet, specifically catered for effective TTC forecasting. In the spatial stream, CollideNet aggregates information for each video frame simultaneously at multiple resolutions. In the temporal stream, along with multi-scale feature encoding, CollideNet also disentangles the non-stationarity, trend, and seasonality components. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in comparison to prior works on three commonly used public datasets, setting a new state-of-the-art by a considerable margin. We conduct cross-dataset evaluations to analyze the generalization capabilities of our method, and visualize the effects of disentanglement of the trend and seasonality components of the video data. We release our code at https://github.com/DeSinister/CollideNet/.

</details>


### [77] [Find, Fix, Reason: Context Repair for Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16243)
*Haojian Huang,Chuanyu Qin,Yinchuan Li,Yingcong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于冻结教师模型提供时空证据补丁的强化学习方法，通过最小干预提升视频推理能力，避免传统方法的知识边界限制和复杂正则化需求


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频推理强化学习方法存在局限性：基于策略的自我探索受限于模型知识边界，混合重放需要复杂正则化，动态上下文方法需要精心设计的预训练和两阶段调优。大模型在指令跟随和多模态理解方面表现优异，能够为小模型提供更丰富的上下文，并通过简单工具快速定位目标区域

Method: 提出观察级干预方法：冻结的工具集成教师识别缺失的时空依赖关系，从原始视频中提供最小证据补丁（时间戳、区域等），学生模型在添加上下文后重新回答。训练采用集成到GRPO中的选择式展开方案，并提出鲁棒改进奖励（RIR），通过正确答案确保结果有效性，通过反映引用证据的推理确保依赖对齐

Result: 在多个相关基准测试中展示了一致的准确性提升和强大的泛化能力

Conclusion: 该方法通过最小干预有效提升视频推理能力，在保持基于策略探索的同时，沿着因果有意义的方向引导学习，对训练堆栈的改动最小

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has advanced video reasoning in large multi-modal models, yet dominant pipelines either rely on on-policy self-exploration, which plateaus at the model's knowledge boundary, or hybrid replay that mixes policies and demands careful regularization. Dynamic context methods zoom into focused evidence but often require curated pretraining and two-stage tuning, and their context remains bounded by a small model's capability. In contrast, larger models excel at instruction following and multi-modal understanding, can supply richer context to smaller models, and rapidly zoom in on target regions via simple tools. Building on this capability, we introduce an observation-level intervention: a frozen, tool-integrated teacher identifies the missing spatiotemporal dependency and provides a minimal evidence patch (e.g., timestamps, regions etc.) from the original video while the question remains unchanged. The student answers again with the added context, and training updates with a chosen-rollout scheme integrated into Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). We further propose a Robust Improvement Reward (RIR) that aligns optimization with two goals: outcome validity through correct answers and dependency alignment through rationales that reflect the cited evidence. Advantages are group-normalized across the batch, preserving on-policy exploration while directing it along causally meaningful directions with minimal changes to the training stack. Experiments on various related benchmarks show consistent accuracy gains and strong generalization. Web page and source code will be available at https://github.com/JethroJames/FFR.git.

</details>


### [78] [Where Do Vision-Language Models Fail? World Scale Analysis for Image Geolocalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16248)
*Siddhant Bharadwaj,Ashish Vashist,Fahimul Aleem,Shruti Vyas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 系统评估多种先进视觉语言模型在仅使用地面视角图像进行国家级别地理定位的零样本能力，揭示模型间显著差异及语义推理在粗粒度地理定位中的潜力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像地理定位方法依赖检索式地点识别或几何视觉定位，而视觉语言模型在多模态任务中展现出强大的零样本推理能力，但其在地理推断方面的性能尚未充分探索

Method: 在零样本设置下评估基于提示的国家预测，不使用图像匹配、GPS元数据或任务特定训练，在三个地理多样性数据集上测试多种最先进的视觉语言模型

Result: 结果显示模型间存在显著差异，语义推理在粗粒度地理定位中具有潜力，但当前视觉语言模型在捕捉细粒度地理线索方面存在局限性

Conclusion: 这是首次针对国家级别地理定位的现代视觉语言模型集中比较，为多模态推理与地理理解交叉领域的研究奠定了基础

Abstract: Image geolocalization has traditionally been addressed through retrieval-based place recognition or geometry-based visual localization pipelines. Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated strong zero-shot reasoning capabilities across multimodal tasks, yet their performance in geographic inference remains underexplored. In this work, we present a systematic evaluation of multiple state-of-the-art VLMs for country-level image geolocalization using ground-view imagery only. Instead of relying on image matching, GPS metadata, or task-specific training, we evaluate prompt-based country prediction in a zero-shot setting. The selected models are tested on three geographically diverse datasets to assess their robustness and generalization ability. Our results reveal substantial variation across models, highlighting the potential of semantic reasoning for coarse geolocalization and the limitations of current VLMs in capturing fine-grained geographic cues. This study provides the first focused comparison of modern VLMs for country-level geolocalization and establishes a foundation for future research at the intersection of multimodal reasoning and geographic understanding.

</details>


### [79] [Seeing the Intangible: Survey of Image Classification into High-Level and Abstract Categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2308.10562)
*Delfina Sol Martinez Pandiani,Valentina Presutti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述论文系统回顾了计算机视觉中高级视觉理解研究，特别关注抽象概念在自动图像分类中的应用，澄清了高级语义的模糊理解，识别了相关任务，并分析了抽象概念处理的挑战与机遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机视觉领域正日益转向"高级"视觉理解任务，但这些任务的确切性质仍不明确且隐含。本文旨在通过系统回顾高级视觉理解研究，特别是抽象概念在自动图像分类中的应用，来澄清这种模糊性。

Method: 采用系统综述方法，对高级视觉理解研究进行多学科分析，将高级语义分类为不同集群（常识、情感、审美、归纳解释语义），识别和分类与高级视觉理解相关的计算机视觉任务，并特别考察抽象概念在CV中的处理方式。

Result: 1. 澄清了CV中高级语义的隐含理解，将其分类为常识、情感、审美和归纳解释语义四大集群；2. 识别并分类了与高级视觉理解相关的计算机视觉任务；3. 揭示了抽象概念图像分类任务中的持续挑战，包括大规模数据集效果有限、需要整合补充信息和中层特征；4. 强调了混合AI系统在处理复杂AC图像分类任务中的重要性。

Conclusion: 该综述增强了我们对计算机视觉中高级视觉推理的理解，为未来研究奠定了基础。特别指出混合AI系统在解决抽象概念图像分类任务多面性方面的重要性，并强调了整合多源信息和特征的必要性。

Abstract: The field of Computer Vision (CV) is increasingly shifting towards ``high-level'' visual sensemaking tasks, yet the exact nature of these tasks remains unclear and tacit. This survey paper addresses this ambiguity by systematically reviewing research on high-level visual understanding, focusing particularly on Abstract Concepts (ACs) in automatic image classification. Our survey contributes in three main ways: Firstly, it clarifies the tacit understanding of high-level semantics in CV through a multidisciplinary analysis, and categorization into distinct clusters, including commonsense, emotional, aesthetic, and inductive interpretative semantics. Secondly, it identifies and categorizes computer vision tasks associated with high-level visual sensemaking, offering insights into the diverse research areas within this domain. Lastly, it examines how abstract concepts such as values and ideologies are handled in CV, revealing challenges and opportunities in AC-based image classification. Notably, our survey of AC image classification tasks highlights persistent challenges, such as the limited efficacy of massive datasets and the importance of integrating supplementary information and mid-level features. We emphasize the growing relevance of hybrid AI systems in addressing the multifaceted nature of AC image classification tasks. Overall, this survey enhances our understanding of high-level visual reasoning in CV and lays the groundwork for future research endeavors.

</details>


### [80] [Do Vision-Language Models Truly Perform Vision Reasoning? A Rigorous Study of the Modality Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16256)
*Yige Xu,Yongjie Wang,Zizhuo Wu,Kaisong Song,Jun Lin,Zhiqi Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CrossMath是一个新颖的多模态推理基准，通过严格控制文本、图像和图文混合三种格式的问题对齐，揭示了当前视觉语言模型主要依赖文本推理而非真正的视觉推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言模型在多种下游任务中表现出色，但尚不清楚其优越性能是源于真正的视觉基础推理，还是主要依赖其文本骨干的推理能力。需要系统性地测量这种跨模态推理差异。

Method: 提出了CrossMath基准，为每个问题构建文本、图像和图文混合三种格式，确保任务相关信息完全相同。通过人工标注验证对齐性，从而有效隔离模态特定的推理差异。使用该基准评估最先进的视觉语言模型，并创建训练集进行微调。

Result: 评估发现：文本推理和视觉推理之间存在显著的性能差距。视觉语言模型在纯文本输入下表现出色，但加入视觉数据（图文混合）通常会导致性能下降。这表明当前模型主要在文本空间进行推理，对视觉证据的依赖有限。通过CrossMath训练集微调后，所有模态的推理性能均显著提升。

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型的推理主要依赖文本空间，而非真正的视觉基础推理。CrossMath基准能够有效测量跨模态推理差异，通过针对性微调可以显著提升模型的视觉推理能力，为开发更平衡的多模态推理系统提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Reasoning in vision-language models (VLMs) has recently attracted significant attention due to its broad applicability across diverse downstream tasks. However, it remains unclear whether the superior performance of VLMs stems from genuine vision-grounded reasoning or relies predominantly on the reasoning capabilities of their textual backbones. To systematically measure this, we introduce CrossMath, a novel multimodal reasoning benchmark designed for controlled cross-modal comparisons. Specifically, we construct each problem in text-only, image-only, and image+text formats guaranteeing identical task-relevant information, verified by human annotators. This rigorous alignment effectively isolates modality-specific reasoning differences while eliminating confounding factors such as information mismatch. Extensive evaluation of state-of-the-art VLMs reveals a consistent phenomenon: a substantial performance gap between textual and visual reasoning. Notably, VLMs excel with text-only inputs, whereas incorporating visual data (image+text) frequently degrades performance compared to the text-only baseline. These findings indicate that current VLMs conduct reasoning primarily in the textual space, with limited genuine reliance on visual evidence. To mitigate this limitation, we curate a CrossMath training set for VLM fine-tuning. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that fine-tuning on this training set significantly boosts reasoning performance across all individual and joint modalities, while yielding robust gains on two general visual reasoning tasks. Source code is available at https://github.com/xuyige/CrossMath.

</details>


### [81] [Information Router for Mitigating Modality Dominance in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16264)
*Seulgi Kim,Mohit Prabhushankar,Ghassan AlRegib*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoIR是一种多模态信息路由方法，通过在融合前显式减少模态间信息差异来解决模态主导问题，而非仅调整注意力分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要通过调整注意力分配来解决模态主导问题，但仅关注模型注意力无法弥补缺失或模糊的信息。现实世界中不同模态的信息密度和信噪比存在差异，单纯调整注意力无法解决信息不足的根本问题。

Method: 提出MoIR（多模态信息路由器），这是一种信息层面的融合方法。它识别信息较少的token，并从信息更强的模态路由补充信息，在大型语言模型处理前构建信息密集的token表示。

Result: 在三个广泛使用的多模态基准测试和多个模型骨干上评估，MoIR始终展现出更平衡的模态贡献，提高了鲁棒性和下游性能，特别是在模态退化情况下表现优异。

Conclusion: 显式修改跨模态信息是缓解多模态推理模型中模态主导问题的有效且互补策略，通过修改信息可用性，MoIR能够实现可靠的模态主导转移，即使某一模态退化时也能保持性能。

Abstract: Vision Language models (VLMs) have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of benchmarks, yet they often suffer from modality dominance, where predictions rely disproportionately on a single modality. Prior approaches primarily address this issue by steering model's attention allocation, implicitly assuming that all modalities provide sufficient information. However, attention only determines where the model focuses, and cannot enrich information that is missing or ambiguous. In the real world, input modalities often differ in information density and their signal-to-noise ratios. In such cases, simply adjusting model's attention does not resolve the underlying lack of information. In this paper, we propose \textsc{MoIR}: \textit{Multi-modal Information Router}, an information-level fusion method that explicitly reduces information disparity prior to fusion. \textsc{MoIR} identifies less informative tokens and routes complementary information from a stronger modality, constructing information-dense token representations before they are processed by a large language model. By modifying information availability, \textsc{MoIR} enables reliable shifts in modality dominance, even when one modality is degraded. We evaluate \textsc{MoIR} on three widely used multi-modal benchmarks across multiple model backbones. Experimental results show that \textsc{MoIR} consistently demonstrates more balanced modality contribution, and improves robustness and downstream performance, particularly even under modality degradation. These findings demonstrate that explicitly modifying cross-modal information is an effective and complementary strategy for mitigating modality dominance in multi-modal reasoning models.

</details>


### [82] [VEFX-Bench: A Holistic Benchmark for Generic Video Editing and Visual Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16272)
*Xiangbo Gao,Sicong Jiang,Bangya Liu,Xinghao Chen,Minglai Yang,Siyuan Yang,Mingyang Wu,Jiongze Yu,Qi Zheng,Haozhi Wang,Jiayi Zhang,Jared Yang,Jie Yang,Zihan Wang,Qing Yin,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VEFX-Dataset：首个大规模人工标注的视频编辑数据集（5,049个样本，9大类32子类），包含三个解耦的质量维度标注；VEFX-Reward：基于该数据集训练的专用视频编辑质量评估奖励模型；VEFX-Bench：包含300个视频-提示对的标准化评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI辅助视频编辑领域缺乏大规模人工标注的完整编辑示例数据集，也缺乏标准化的评估系统。现有资源规模小、缺少编辑输出或人工质量标签，评估多依赖昂贵的人工检查或非专用的通用视觉语言模型。

Method: 1) 构建VEFX-Dataset：包含5,049个视频编辑示例，涵盖9个主要编辑类别和32个子类别，每个样本在三个解耦维度（指令遵循、渲染质量、编辑排他性）进行人工标注；2) 开发VEFX-Reward：基于VEFX-Dataset训练的奖励模型，联合处理源视频、编辑指令和编辑后视频，通过序数回归预测各维度质量分数；3) 发布VEFX-Bench：包含300个精选视频-提示对的标准化评测基准。

Result: VEFX-Reward在标准IQA/VQA指标和组间偏好评估中比通用VLM评估器和现有奖励模型更符合人类判断。使用VEFX-Reward评估代表性商业和开源视频编辑系统，揭示了当前模型在视觉合理性、指令遵循和编辑局部性方面存在持续差距。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了视频编辑领域大规模标注数据集和专用评估工具的空白。VEFX-Dataset、VEFX-Reward和VEFX-Bench为视频编辑系统的开发、评估和比较提供了标准化基础设施，有助于推动AI辅助视频编辑技术的发展。

Abstract: As AI-assisted video creation becomes increasingly practical, instruction-guided video editing has become essential for refining generated or captured footage to meet professional requirements. Yet the field still lacks both a large-scale human-annotated dataset with complete editing examples and a standardized evaluator for comparing editing systems. Existing resources are limited by small scale, missing edited outputs, or the absence of human quality labels, while current evaluation often relies on expensive manual inspection or generic vision-language model judges that are not specialized for editing quality. We introduce VEFX-Dataset, a human-annotated dataset containing 5,049 video editing examples across 9 major editing categories and 32 subcategories, each labeled along three decoupled dimensions: Instruction Following, Rendering Quality, and Edit Exclusivity. Building on VEFX-Dataset, we propose VEFX-Reward, a reward model designed specifically for video editing quality assessment. VEFX-Reward jointly processes the source video, the editing instruction, and the edited video, and predicts per-dimension quality scores via ordinal regression. We further release VEFX-Bench, a benchmark of 300 curated video-prompt pairs for standardized comparison of editing systems. Experiments show that VEFX-Reward aligns more strongly with human judgments than generic VLM judges and prior reward models on both standard IQA/VQA metrics and group-wise preference evaluation. Using VEFX-Reward as an evaluator, we benchmark representative commercial and open-source video editing systems, revealing a persistent gap between visual plausibility, instruction following, and edit locality in current models.

</details>


### [83] [Enhancing Hazy Wildlife Imagery: AnimalHaze3k and IncepDehazeGan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16284)
*Shivarth Rai,Tejeswar Pokuri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了AnimalHaze3k合成数据集和IncepDehazeGan架构，用于解决野生动物图像去雾问题，显著提升下游检测任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气雾霾严重降低野生动物图像质量，阻碍了用于保护的关键计算机视觉应用（如动物检测、跟踪和行为分析），需要开发有效的去雾方法。

Method: 1. 创建AnimalHaze3k合成数据集：包含3,477张雾霾图像，基于1,159张清晰野生动物照片通过物理基础流程生成；2. 提出新颖的IncepDehazeGan架构：在GAN框架中结合inception块和残差跳跃连接。

Result: 1. 去雾性能达到SOTA：SSIM 0.8914，PSNR 20.54，LPIPS 0.1104，比竞争方法SSIM高6.27%，PSNR高10.2%；2. 下游检测任务提升：去雾图像使YOLOv11检测mAP提高112%，IoU提高67%。

Conclusion: 该方法为生态学家在挑战性环境条件下提供了可靠的人口监测和监视工具，展示了通过鲁棒视觉分析增强野生动物保护工作的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Atmospheric haze significantly degrades wildlife imagery, impeding computer vision applications critical for conservation, such as animal detection, tracking, and behavior analysis. To address this challenge, we introduce AnimalHaze3k a synthetic dataset comprising of 3,477 hazy images generated from 1,159 clear wildlife photographs through a physics-based pipeline. Our novel IncepDehazeGan architecture combines inception blocks with residual skip connections in a GAN framework, achieving state-of-the-art performance (SSIM: 0.8914, PSNR: 20.54, and LPIPS: 0.1104), delivering 6.27% higher SSIM and 10.2% better PSNR than competing approaches. When applied to downstream detection tasks, dehazed images improved YOLOv11 detection mAP by 112% and IoU by 67%. These advances can provide ecologists with reliable tools for population monitoring and surveillance in challenging environmental conditions, demonstrating significant potential for enhancing wildlife conservation efforts through robust visual analytics.

</details>


### [84] [FineCog-Nav: Integrating Fine-grained Cognitive Modules for Zero-shot Multimodal UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16298)
*Dian Shao,Zhengzheng Xu,Peiyang Wang,Like Liu,Yule Wang,Jieqi Shi,Jing Huo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FineCog-Nav是一个受人类认知启发的无人机视觉语言导航框架，通过细粒度模块化架构实现零样本导航，在AerialVLN-Fine基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本无人机视觉语言导航方法存在局限性：依赖大型基础模型、使用通用提示、模块间协调松散。需要更精细的认知架构来提升导航性能。

Method: 提出FineCog-Nav框架，采用自上而下的认知架构，将导航分解为语言处理、感知、注意力、记忆、想象、推理和决策等细粒度模块。每个模块由中等规模的基础模型驱动，使用角色特定提示和结构化输入输出协议。

Result: 构建AerialVLN-Fine基准（300条轨迹），包含句子级指令-轨迹对齐和包含明确视觉终点及地标参考的细化指令。实验表明FineCog-Nav在指令遵循、长视野规划和未见环境泛化方面优于零样本基线方法。

Conclusion: 细粒度认知模块化对零样本空中导航具有有效性，FineCog-Nav框架通过模块化认知架构提升了导航性能和可解释性。

Abstract: UAV vision-language navigation (VLN) requires an agent to navigate complex 3D environments from an egocentric perspective while following ambiguous multi-step instructions over long horizons. Existing zero-shot methods remain limited, as they often rely on large base models, generic prompts, and loosely coordinated modules. In this work, we propose FineCog-Nav, a top-down framework inspired by human cognition that organizes navigation into fine-grained modules for language processing, perception, attention, memory, imagination, reasoning, and decision-making. Each module is driven by a moderate-sized foundation model with role-specific prompts and structured input-output protocols, enabling effective collaboration and improved interpretability. To support fine-grained evaluation, we construct AerialVLN-Fine, a curated benchmark of 300 trajectories derived from AerialVLN, with sentence-level instruction-trajectory alignment and refined instructions containing explicit visual endpoints and landmark references. Experiments show that FineCog-Nav consistently outperforms zero-shot baselines in instruction adherence, long-horizon planning, and generalization to unseen environments. These results suggest the effectiveness of fine-grained cognitive modularization for zero-shot aerial navigation. Project page: https://smartdianlab.github.io/projects-FineCogNav.

</details>


### [85] [Repurposing 3D Generative Model for Autoregressive Layout Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16299)
*Haoran Feng,Yifan Niu,Zehuan Huang,Yang-Tian Sun,Chunchao Guo,Yuxin Peng,Lu Sheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LaviGen是一个将3D生成模型重新用于3D布局生成的框架，通过自回归过程在原生3D空间中建模几何关系和物理约束，生成连贯且物理合理的3D场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常从文本描述推断物体布局，而LaviGen旨在直接在原生3D空间中操作，更有效地建模几何关系和物理约束，以生成更连贯和物理合理的3D场景。

Method: 1. 将布局生成建模为自回归过程，显式建模物体间的几何关系和物理约束；2. 提出适配的3D扩散模型，整合场景、物体和指令信息；3. 采用双重引导自展开蒸馏机制提高效率和空间准确性。

Result: 在LayoutVLM基准测试中，LaviGen实现了优越的3D布局生成性能：物理合理性比现有最佳方法高19%，计算速度提升65%。

Conclusion: LaviGen通过重新利用3D生成模型，在原生3D空间中直接生成布局，有效建模几何和物理约束，显著提升了3D布局生成的物理合理性和计算效率。

Abstract: We introduce LaviGen, a framework that repurposes 3D generative models for 3D layout generation. Unlike previous methods that infer object layouts from textual descriptions, LaviGen operates directly in the native 3D space, formulating layout generation as an autoregressive process that explicitly models geometric relations and physical constraints among objects, producing coherent and physically plausible 3D scenes. To further enhance this process, we propose an adapted 3D diffusion model that integrates scene, object, and instruction information and employs a dual-guidance self-rollout distillation mechanism to improve efficiency and spatial accuracy. Extensive experiments on the LayoutVLM benchmark show LaviGen achieves superior 3D layout generation performance, with 19% higher physical plausibility than the state of the art and 65% faster computation. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/fenghora/LaviGen.

</details>


### [86] [Early Detection of Acute Myeloid Leukemia (AML) Using YOLOv12 Deep Learning Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16082)
*Enas E. Ahmed,Salah A. Aly,Mayar Moner*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 使用YOLOv12深度学习模型对急性髓系白血病细胞进行多分类，结合细胞和细胞核特征分割方法，其中基于细胞分割的Otsu阈值处理取得了99.3%的最高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急性髓系白血病是一种危及生命的血癌，由于不同细胞类型在视觉上相似，其准确分类一直是一个具有挑战性的任务。本研究旨在解决AML细胞的多分类问题。

Method: 采用YOLOv12深度学习模型进行AML细胞分类。应用了两种基于细胞和细胞核特征的分割方法，使用Hue通道和Otsu阈值技术对图像进行预处理。比较了不同分割策略对分类性能的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明，YOLOv12结合基于细胞分割的Otsu阈值处理方法取得了最佳性能，验证集和测试集准确率均达到99.3%。

Conclusion: YOLOv12模型结合适当的图像预处理技术能够有效实现AML细胞的高精度多分类，为白血病诊断提供了有前景的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Acute Myeloid Leukemia (AML) is one of the most life-threatening type of blood cancers, and its accurate classification is considered and remains a challenging task due to the visual similarity between various cell types. This study addresses the classification of the multiclasses of AML cells Utilizing YOLOv12 deep learning model. We applied two segmentation approaches based on cell and nucleus features, using Hue channel and Otsu thresholding techniques to preprocess the images prior to classification. Our experiments demonstrate that YOLOv12 with Otsu thresholding on cell-based segmentation achieved the highest level of validation and test accuracy, both reaching 99.3%.

</details>

### [87] [An Agentic Workflow for Detecting Personally Identifiable Information in Crash Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15369)
*Junyi Ma,Pei Li,Rui Gan,Kai Cheng,Steven T. Parker,Bin Ran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 开发了一个本地可部署的智能工作流，用于检测交通事故报告中的个人身份信息，结合规则模型和微调LLM，通过集成提取和验证机制实现高精度PII检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通事故报告中的叙述包含重要的安全分析信息，但广泛使用受到个人身份信息（PII）的限制。PII在叙述中稀疏且不一致地出现，手动检测不可扩展，现有基于规则的方法无法捕捉上下文相关的PII。

Method: 开发了一个本地可部署的智能工作流，包含混合提取器和验证器。混合提取器将结构化PII（如电话号码、电子邮件）路由到基于规则的模型（Presidio），将上下文相关的PII（如姓名、家庭地址、字母数字标识符）路由到领域适应、微调的大型语言模型。为处理具有挑战性类别的模糊性，工作流集成了集成LLM提取和智能验证步骤，通过基于证据的推理过滤误检测。

Result: 在真实交通事故数据集上评估，智能工作流实现了精确率0.82、召回率0.94、F1分数0.87和准确率0.96的强性能，优于多个基线方法。消融结果表明，集成LLM提取和验证器对家庭地址和字母数字标识符的检测有改进。工作流在本地运行，支持隐私敏感的操作环境。

Conclusion: 该工作为可扩展、隐私保护的交通事故数据处理提供了实用且稳健的路径，支持更广泛的研究和安全干预，同时保护个人隐私。

Abstract: Crash narratives in crash reports provide crucial contextual information for traffic safety analysis. Yet, their broader use is hindered by the presence of personally identifiable information (PII), including names, home addresses, and license plate numbers. Because PII appears sparsely and inconsistently in crash narratives, manual detection is not scalable, and existing rule-based approaches often fail to capture context-dependent PII. This study develops and evaluates a locally deployable, agentic workflow for PII detection in crash narratives by leveraging large language models (LLMs). The workflow contains a Hybrid Extractor and a Verifier. The Hybrid Extractor routes structured PII (e.g., phone numbers and email addresses) to a rule-based model (i.e., Presidio) and context-dependent PII (e.g., names, home addresses, and alphanumeric identifiers) to a domain-adapted, fine-tuned LLM. To address ambiguity in challenging categories, the workflow incorporates ensemble LLM extraction and an agentic verification step that filters false detections through evidence-based reasoning. Evaluated on a real-world crash dataset, the agentic workflow achieves strong performance with a precision of 0.82, a recall of 0.94, an F1 of 0.87, and an accuracy of 0.96, outperforming multiple baseline methods. Moreover, the ablation results suggest that ensemble LLM extraction and Verifier offer improved detection for home addresses and alphanumeric identifiers. The workflow runs locally, supporting privacy-sensitive operational settings where external APIs are restricted. This work offers a practical and robust path for scalable, privacy-preserving crash data processing, enabling broader research and safety interventions while safeguarding individual privacy.

</details>


### [88] [TopFeaRe: Locating Critical State of Adversarial Resilience for Graphs Regarding Topology-Feature Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15370)
*Xinxin Fan,Wenxiong Chen,Quanliang Jing,Chi Lin,Shaoye Luo,Wenbo Song,Yunfeng Lu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于复杂动态系统平衡点理论的图对抗防御方法，通过定位图的对抗弹性临界状态来防御拓扑和特征攻击


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法未能揭示图对抗攻击中拓扑和特征两个维度攻击的必要性及其融合机制，需要从内在机理层面理解图对抗弹性

Method: 1) 将图对抗攻击建模为复杂动态系统的振荡行为；2) 设计二维拓扑-特征纠缠函数表示扰动图；3) 利用平衡点理论定位图的对抗弹性临界状态

Result: 在五个常用真实数据集上的多角度实验验证了方法的有效性，在四种代表性图对抗攻击下显著优于现有最先进基线方法

Conclusion: 通过复杂动态系统理论为图对抗防御提供了新的理论框架，能够有效定位图的对抗弹性临界状态，显著提升防御性能

Abstract: Graph adversarial attacks are usually produced from the two perspectives of topology/structure and node feature, both of them represent the paramount characteristics learned by today's deep learning models. Although some defense countermeasures are proposed at present, they fails to disclose the intrinsic reasons why these two aspects necessitate and how they are adequately fused to co-learn the graph representation. Towards this question, we in this paper propose an adversarial defense approach through locating the graph's critical state of adversarial resilience, resorting to the equilibrium-point theory in the discipline of complex dynamic system (CDS). In brief, our work has three novelties: i) Adversarial-Attack Modeling, i.e. map a graph regime into CDS, and use the oscillation of dynamic system to model the behavior of adversarial perturbation; ii) 2D Topology-Feature-Entangled Function Design for Perturbed Graph, i.e. project graph topology and node feature as two characteristic spaces, and define two-dimensional entangled perturbation functions to represent the dynamic variance under adversarial attacks; and iii) Location of Critical State of Adversarial Resilience, i.e. utilize the equilibrium-point theory to locate the graph's critical state of attack resilience resorting to the perturbation-reflected 2D function. Finally, multi-facet experiments on five commonly-used realistic datasets validate the effectiveness of our proposed approach, and the results show our approach can significantly outperform the state-of-the-art baselines under four representative graph adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [89] [The Synthetic Media Shift: Tracking the Rise, Virality, and Detectability of AI-Generated Multimodal Misinformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15372)
*Zacharias Chrysidis,Stefanos-Iordanis Papadopoulos,Symeon Papadopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CONVEX数据集包含15万+多模态虚假信息帖子，分析显示AI生成内容传播性高但主要依赖被动参与，检测模型性能随时间下降


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI发展，真实与合成媒体界限模糊，威胁在线信息完整性，需要研究多模态虚假信息的传播特性和检测挑战

Method: 构建CONVEX大规模多模态虚假信息数据集（包含误配图、编辑和AI生成内容），分析传播动态、参与模式和共识形成，评估专业检测器和视觉语言模型性能

Result: AI生成内容传播性高但主要依赖被动参与；初始报告较慢但一旦标记后社区共识形成更快；检测模型性能随时间持续下降

Conclusion: 数字信息环境快速演变，需要持续监控和适应性策略来应对多模态虚假信息挑战，特别是AI生成内容的检测和治理

Abstract: As generative AI advances, the distinction between authentic and synthetic media is increasingly blurred, challenging the integrity of online information. In this study, we present CONVEX, a large-scale dataset of multimodal misinformation involving miscaptioned, edited, and AI-generated visual content, comprising over 150K multimodal posts with associated notes and engagement metrics from X's Community Notes. We analyze how multimodal misinformation evolves in terms of virality, engagement, and consensus dynamics, with a focus on synthetic media. Our results show that while AI-generated content achieves disproportionate virality, its spread is driven primarily by passive engagement rather than active discourse. Despite slower initial reporting, AI-generated content reaches community consensus more quickly once flagged. Moreover, our evaluation of specialized detectors and vision-language models reveals a consistent decline in performance over time in distinguishing synthetic from authentic images as generative models evolve. These findings highlight the need for continuous monitoring and adaptive strategies in the rapidly evolving digital information environment.

</details>


### [90] [Graded Symbolic Verification with a Fuzzy Dolev-Yao Attacker Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15402)
*Murat Moran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种用于分析协议累积侧信道泄漏的渐近攻击者知识模型，取代了传统的二元（已知/未知）Dolev-Yao模型，通过实验显示传统安全协议在考虑累积泄漏时会失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Dolev-Yao符号协议验证使用二元攻击者知识模型（已知/未知），这种抽象忽略了累积侧信道设置，其中重复的噪声观测会逐步改善攻击者知识。需要一种能够建模渐进知识获取过程的更精细模型。

Method: 使用分级攻击者视图μ_K∈[0,1]建模攻击者知识，采用乘积T-范数泄漏更新机制，在Modified Murphi中实现有限网格显式状态执行。方法通过精确概念格属性约简进行优化，并暴露阈值驱动的安全到失败转换。

Result: 在对称和非对称协议（包括Needham-Schroeder-Lowe）上的执行结果表明，在清晰语义下通过基线模型的协议，一旦启用累积侧信道泄漏就会失败。该方法能够识别在相应二元运行中未表示的阈值驱动转换。

Conclusion: 累积侧信道泄漏对协议安全性有显著影响，传统二元Dolev-Yao模型不足以捕捉这种渐进知识获取过程。提出的分级知识模型和优化方法能够更准确地评估协议在现实侧信道攻击下的安全性。

Abstract: Classical symbolic protocol verification under Dolev--Yao uses binary attacker knowledge (known/unknown). This abstraction misses cumulative side-channel settings, where repeated noisy observations progressively improve attacker knowledge. We model this process with a graded attacker view \(μ_K\in[0,1]\), product T-norm leak updates, and finite-grid explicit-state execution in Modified Murphi.
  The method is optimised with exact concept-lattice attribute reducts and exposes threshold-driven safe-to-fail transitions that are not represented in corresponding binary runs under the same bounded assumptions. Executed results on symmetric and asymmetric protocols, including Needham--Schroeder--Lowe (NSL), show that baseline models passing under crisp semantics can fail once cumulative side-channel leakage is enabled.

</details>


### [91] [SecureRouter: Encrypted Routing for Efficient Secure Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15499)
*Yukuan Zhang,Mengxin Zheng,Qian Lou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SecureRouter：一个端到端加密路由和推理框架，通过加密下的输入自适应模型选择来加速安全Transformer推理，相比先前工作实现1.95倍延迟降低


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于安全多方计算（MPC）的隐私保护推理系统使用单一固定模型处理所有加密输入，忽略了不同输入需要不同模型规模来平衡效率与精度，导致推理速度慢、成本高，限制了实际部署

Method: 1）建立统一加密管道，集成安全路由器和MPC优化模型池；2）训练阶段：MPC成本感知的安全路由器从加密特征预测每个模型的效用和成本；3）推理阶段：MPC优化模型池的架构和量化方案协同训练以最小化MPC通信和计算开销

Result: 相比先前工作，SecureRouter实现了1.95倍的延迟降低，且精度损失可忽略不计，为可扩展高效的安全AI推理提供了实用路径

Conclusion: SecureRouter通过输入自适应模型选择和端到端加密管道，显著加速了安全Transformer推理，在保持完全数据和模型机密性的同时，平衡了效率与精度，推动了隐私保护AI的实际部署

Abstract: Cryptographically secure neural network inference typically relies on secure computing techniques such as Secure Multi-Party Computation (MPC), enabling cloud servers to process client inputs without decrypting them. Although prior privacy-preserving inference systems co-design network optimizations with MPC, they remain slow and costly, limiting real-world deployment. A major bottleneck is their use of a single, fixed transformer model for all encrypted inputs, ignoring that different inputs require different model sizes to balance efficiency and accuracy. We present SecureRouter, an end-to-end encrypted routing and inference framework that accelerates secure transformer inference through input-adaptive model selection under encryption. SecureRouter establishes a unified encrypted pipeline that integrates a secure router with an MPC-optimized model pool, enabling coordinated routing, inference, and protocol execution while preserving full data and model confidentiality. The framework includes training-phase and inference-phase components: an MPC-cost-aware secure router that predicts per-model utility and cost from encrypted features, and an MPC-optimized model pool whose architectures and quantization schemes are co-trained to minimize MPC communication and computation overhead. Compared to prior work, SecureRouter achieves a latency reduction by 1.95x with negligible accuracy loss, offering a practical path toward scalable and efficient secure AI inference. Our open-source implementation is available at: https://github.com/UCF-ML-Research/SecureRouter

</details>


### [92] [CSLE: A Reinforcement Learning Platform for Autonomous Security Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15590)
*Kim Hammar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CSLE是一个用于自主安全管理的强化学习平台，通过结合仿真和仿真系统在接近真实操作环境的条件下进行实验，解决了现有方案局限于仿真环境的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习在安全管理中的应用大多局限于仿真环境，不清楚它们如何推广到实际操作系统。需要一种能够在接近真实条件下进行实验的平台来弥合理论性能与实际操作性能之间的差距。

Method: CSLE平台包含两个系统：1）仿真系统，在虚拟化环境中复制目标系统的关键组件，用于收集测量数据和日志，并识别系统模型（如马尔可夫决策过程）；2）仿真系统，通过系统模型的仿真高效学习安全策略，然后在仿真系统中评估和优化这些策略。

Result: 通过四个用例（流量控制、复制控制、分段控制和恢复控制）展示了CSLE能够在一个近似操作系统的环境中实现接近最优的安全管理。

Conclusion: CSLE平台通过结合仿真和仿真系统，为自主安全管理提供了一个能够在接近真实条件下进行实验的强化学习平台，有效弥合了理论性能与实际操作性能之间的差距。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is a promising approach to autonomous and adaptive security management in networked systems. However, current reinforcement learning solutions for security management are mostly limited to simulation environments and it is unclear how they generalize to operational systems. In this paper, we address this limitation by presenting CSLE: a reinforcement learning platform for autonomous security management that enables experimentation under realistic conditions. Conceptually, CSLE encompasses two systems. First, it includes an emulation system that replicates key components of the target system in a virtualized environment. We use this system to gather measurements and logs, based on which we identify a system model, such as a Markov decision process. Second, it includes a simulation system where security strategies are efficiently learned through simulations of the system model. The learned strategies are then evaluated and refined in the emulation system to close the gap between theoretical and operational performance. We demonstrate CSLE through four use cases: flow control, replication control, segmentation control, and recovery control. Through these use cases, we show that CSLE enables near-optimal security management in an environment that approximates an operational system.

</details>


### [93] [Privacy, Prediction, and Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15596)
*Ben Jacobsen,Nitin Kohli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在满足差分隐私约束下的援助资源分配系统，分析了隐私保护与个体/单元级目标定位之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法预测越来越多地用于稀缺资源分配，但最近研究表明简单的单元级分配策略可能优于个体级目标定位。同时，隐私问题也使单元级目标定位成为隐私和效用的双重推荐方案。作者希望全面理解隐私保护与目标定位级别之间的相互作用。

Method: 研究了满足差分隐私的援助分配系统，将现有的私有优化工作与非私有文献中的经济分配模型相结合。分析了在随机和无分布设置下，个体级和单元级分配策略的私有变体，考虑了数据可用性的各种约束条件。

Result: 通过分析提供了清晰、可解释的边界，描述了隐私、效率和目标定位精度在分配中的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该研究系统性地探索了隐私保护与资源分配目标定位级别之间的相互作用，为在隐私约束下设计有效的援助分配系统提供了理论框架和量化分析工具。

Abstract: Algorithmic predictions are increasingly used to inform the allocation of scarce resources. The promise of these methods is that, through machine learning, they can better identify the people who would benefit most from interventions. Recently, however, several works have called this assumption into question by demonstrating the existence of settings where simple, unit-level allocation strategies can meet or even exceed the performance of those based on individual-level targeting. Separately, other works have objected to individual-level targeting on privacy grounds, leading to an unusual situation where a single solution, unit-level targeting, is recommended for reasons of both privacy and utility. Motivated by the desire to fully understand the interplay of privacy and targeting levels, we initiate the study of aid allocation systems that satisfy differential privacy, synthesizing existing works on private optimization with the economic models of aid allocation used in the non-private literature. To this end, we investigate private variants of both individual and unit-level allocation strategies in both stochastic and distribution-free settings under a range of constraints on data availability. Through this analysis, we provide clean, interpretable bounds characterizing the tradeoffs between privacy, efficiency, and targeting precision in allocation.

</details>


### [94] [Too Private to Tell: Practical Token Theft Attacks on Apple Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15637)
*Haoling Zhou,Shixuan Zhao,Chao Wang,Zhiqiang Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究人员发现了苹果智能服务令牌机制中的漏洞，通过Serpent攻击可实现跨设备令牌重放，允许攻击者窃取受害者令牌并在其他设备上使用，绕过了使用限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 苹果智能声称通过匿名访问令牌的两阶段认证授权设计特别关注用户安全和隐私。本研究旨在通过流量分析、逆向工程和与公开文档的交叉比对，揭示该令牌发放机制中可能存在的漏洞。

Method: 采用流量分析、逆向工程和与苹果公开文档的交叉比对方法，研究苹果智能的令牌发放机制。具体提出了Serpent攻击——首个针对苹果智能的实用跨设备令牌重放攻击。

Result: 成功在最新macOS 26 Tahoe上实现攻击，证明攻击者即使已用尽自身配额，也能立即重新获得苹果智能服务访问权限。已向厂商负责任地披露漏洞，获得苹果确认并分配了CVE编号和赏金。

Conclusion: 匿名化身份本身并不能确保AI服务安全；强制执行不可转移性需要与合法用户进行加密绑定。这为内置AI服务提供了普遍教训。

Abstract: Apple Intelligence is a generative AI (GenAI) service provided by Apple on its devices. While offering a similar set of features as other similar GenAI services, Apple Intelligence is claimed to be designed with an extra focus on user security and privacy through a two-stage authentication and authorization design using anonymous access tokens. In this paper, we present our investigation into this token issuance mechanism with a goal to reveal possible vulnerabilities using traffic analysis, reverse engineering, and cross comparison with Apple's public documentation. Specifically, we present the Serpent attack, the first practical cross-device token replay attack against Apple Intelligence that allows the attacker to steal the access tokens from the victim's device and utilise them on a different device, with all usage rate-limited against the victim. We have achieved successful attacks on the latest macOS 26 Tahoe and demonstrated that an attacker, who even has used up its own allowance, can immediately regain access to Apple Intelligence service. We have responsibly disclosed the vulnerabilities to the vendors and received confirmation from Apple with CVE assigned and bounty given. Our results highlight a general lesson for built-in AI services: Anonymising identity does not by itself make the AI service secure; Enforcing non-transferability requires cryptographic binding to the rightful user.

</details>


### [95] [Half-Moon Cookie: Private, Similarity-Based Blocklisting with TOCTOU-Attack Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15641)
*Xinyuan Zhang,Anrin Chakraborti,Michael K. Reiter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Half-Moon Cookie是一个私有黑名单框架，允许客户端在不泄露查询或黑名单内容的情况下检查项目是否接近黑名单中的元素


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑名单技术要么需要公开黑名单，要么需要客户端向服务器泄露查询内容，存在隐私泄露问题

Method: 将嵌入步骤与黑名单检查分离，支持高效的项目检查以及先前检查结果的验证，防止TOCTOU攻击

Result: 实现了基于相似性的恶意软件检测，能够有效识别恶意可执行文件，同时保护客户端输入和黑名单隐私

Conclusion: Half-Moon Cookie框架在保持隐私保护的同时，提供了实用的黑名单检查解决方案，特别适用于恶意软件检测等安全应用

Abstract: Blocklisting is a common technique for preventing the use of known malicious content. However, conventional blocklisting infrastructures require either the blocklist to be public or clients to reveal their queries to the blocklist server. In this work, we introduce a private blocklisting framework, Half-Moon Cookie, by which a client can check an item against a proprietary blocklist held by a server, to determine whether the item is close to any blocklist element in a metric space. Critically, our design separates the embedding step from the blocklist check, so that performance degrades with their sum and not their product. Still, this check might be too costly to perform on the critical path of using the item, and so our design also supports a very efficient check that an item previously passed the blocklist check. In doing so, we support applications where one client can perform the blocklist check on the item before sending it, and recipients can more efficiently confirm the previous result before using the item, thereby avoiding TOCTOU attacks. We demonstrate how Half-Moon Cookie can be instantiated for similarity-based malware detection, enabling effective identification of malicious executables without revealing client inputs or disclosing the underlying blocklist.

</details>


### [96] [DPDSyn: Improving Differentially Private Dataset Synthesis for Model Training by Downstream Task Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15660)
*Mingxuan Jia,Wen Huang,Weixin Zhao,Xingyi Wang,Jian Peng,Zhishuo Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出DPDSyn方法，通过训练满足差分隐私的AI模型来合成数据集，相比现有方法在数据效用和合成效率上有显著提升


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私数据集合成方法通过选择低维分布来近似原始数据集分布，但难以选择合适的低维分布，导致合成数据集的数据效用降低。需要一种既能保护隐私又能保持数据效用的新方法。

Method: 提出DPDSyn方法：1）在原始私有数据集上训练满足差分隐私的AI模型用于下游任务；2）利用训练好的模型合成数据集。模型满足差分隐私确保隐私保护，同时模型为下游任务训练保留了关键信息。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验表明，DPDSyn在准确率上最大提升2.40倍，合成效率最大提升333.73倍，优于八个最先进的基线方法。进一步实验验证了DPDSyn在不同数据规模下具有良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: DPDSyn通过训练差分隐私AI模型来合成数据集，既能有效保护隐私，又能保持较高的数据效用，在准确率和合成效率方面显著优于现有方法，具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: How to synthesize a dataset while achieving differential privacy for AI model training is a meaningful but challenging problem. To address this problem, state-of-the-art methods first select original private dataset's multiple low-dimensional distributions that have the potential to approximate the distribution of original private dataset with high precision, and then synthesize a dataset obeying all selected low-dimensional distributions as the synthetic dataset. However, it is difficult to select suitable low-dimensional distributions, which in turn degrades the data utility of resulting synthetic dataset. To improve differentially private dataset synthesis, we propose to train a differentially private AI model for downstream tasks on the original private dataset and utilize the trained model to synthesize datasets. In particular, on the one hand, the AI model satisfies differential privacy so no matter how to use the model does not disclose private information of original private dataset. On the other hand, the AI model is trained to complete the downstream task so the AI model preserves critical information for completing downstream tasks. We utilize the AI model to synthesize datasets to achieve the goal of improving data utility while preserving privacy. Empirical evaluations on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that our proposed DPDSyn consistently outperforms eight state-of-the-art baselines with a maximum improvement of 2.40x in accuracy and 333.73x in synthesis efficiency. Further experiments also validate that DPDSyn has strong scalability across varying data scales.

</details>


### [97] [Into the Gray Zone: Domain Contexts Can Blur LLM Safety Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15717)
*Ki Sen Hung,Xi Yang,Chang Liu,Haoran Li,Kejiang Chen,Changxuan Fan,Tsun On Kwok,Weiming Zhang,Xiaomeng Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Jargon框架利用安全研究上下文和多轮对抗交互，通过领域特定语境选择性放松防御的漏洞，在包括GPT-5.2、Claude-4.5、Gemini-3在内的七大前沿模型中实现超过93%的攻击成功率，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM对齐需要在帮助性和无害性之间取得平衡，但当同一知识既可用于合法目的也可用于恶意目的时，这两个目标会产生冲突。研究发现领域特定上下文（如化学）会选择性放松对领域相关有害知识的防御，而安全研究上下文（如越狱研究）会触发对所有伤害类别的广泛放松，这种上下文敏感的对齐机制存在系统性漏洞。

Method: 提出Jargon框架，结合安全研究上下文和多轮对抗交互来系统性地利用对齐漏洞。通过激活空间分析揭示Jargon查询占据良性输入和有害输入之间的中间区域（灰色地带），其中拒绝决策变得不可靠。为缓解此漏洞，设计了策略引导的安全保障机制，通过对齐微调将这种能力内化。

Result: Jargon在七大前沿模型（包括GPT-5.2、Claude-4.5、Gemini-3）中实现了超过93%的攻击成功率，显著优于现有方法。激活空间分析显示Jargon查询位于良性输入和有害输入之间的中间区域。通过策略引导的安全保障机制和对齐微调，在保持帮助性的同时有效降低了攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 上下文敏感的对齐机制存在系统性漏洞，Jargon框架能有效利用这些漏洞实现高成功率攻击。激活空间分析揭示了拒绝决策在灰色地带的不可靠性。提出的策略引导安全保障机制和对齐微调方法能有效缓解此漏洞，为LLM对齐提供了新的防御思路。

Abstract: A central goal of LLM alignment is to balance helpfulness with harmlessness, yet these objectives conflict when the same knowledge serves both legitimate and malicious purposes. This tension is amplified by context-sensitive alignment: we observe that domain-specific contexts (e.g., chemistry) selectively relax defenses for domain-relevant harmful knowledge, while safety-research contexts (e.g., jailbreak studies) trigger broader relaxation spanning all harm categories. To systematically exploit this vulnerability, we propose Jargon, a framework combining safety-research contexts with multi-turn adversarial interactions that achieves attack success rates exceeding 93% across seven frontier models, including GPT-5.2, Claude-4.5, and Gemini-3, substantially outperforming existing methods. Activation space analysis reveals that Jargon queries occupy an intermediate region between benign and harmful inputs, a gray zone where refusal decisions become unreliable. To mitigate this vulnerability, we design a policy-guided safeguard that steers models toward helpful yet harmless responses, and internalize this capability through alignment fine-tuning, reducing attack success rates while preserving helpfulness.

</details>


### [98] [Privacy-Preserving LLMs Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15728)
*Xidong Wu,Yukuan Zhang,Yuqiong Ji,Reza Shirkavand,Qian Lou,Shangqian Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PPRoute是一个保护隐私的LLM路由框架，通过MPC友好操作、多步训练算法和O(1)通信复杂度的Top-k算法，在保证路由质量的同时实现20倍加速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM路由在用户和LLM之间增加了中间层，带来了新的隐私风险，而现有的隐私保护技术如MPC存在计算开销过高的问题，需要设计高效的隐私保护路由框架

Method: 1) 使用MPC友好操作加速编码器推理；2) 多步模型训练算法在加密域约束下保持路由质量；3) 提出通信复杂度O(1)的未排序Top-k算法用于安全模型搜索

Result: 在不同数据集上，PPRoute实现了与明文对应方案相当的性能，同时比朴素MPC实现加速约20倍

Conclusion: PPRoute框架有效解决了LLM路由中的隐私保护问题，在保证路由质量的同时显著降低了MPC的计算开销，为隐私保护LLM路由提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing has emerged as a critical strategy to balance model performance and cost-efficiency by dynamically selecting services from various model providers. However, LLM routing adds an intermediate layer between users and LLMs, creating new privacy risks to user data. These privacy risks have not been systematically studied. Although cryptographic techniques such as Secure Multi-Party Computation (MPC) enable privacy-preserving computation, their protocol design and implementation remain under-explored, and naïve implementations typically incur prohibitive computational overhead. To address this, we propose a privacy-preserving LLM routing framework (PPRoute). PPRoute includes multiple strategies to speed up encoder inference and nearest neighbor search under the MPC and maintain the quality of LLM routing. First, PPRoute uses MPC-friendly operations to boost the encoder inference. Second, PPRoute uses a multiple-step model training algorithm to maintain routing quality despite the constraints of the encrypted domain. Third, PPRoute proposes an unsorted Top-k algorithm with $O(1)$ communication complexity for secure sorting in model search, significantly reducing communication latency. Across different datasets, PPRoute achieves the performance of plaintext counterparts, while achieving approximately a 20$\times$ speedup over naïve MPC implementations.

</details>


### [99] [Secure Authentication in Wireless IoT: Hamming Code Assisted SRAM PUF as Device Fingerprint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15810)
*Florian Lehn,Pascal Ahr,Hans D. Schotten*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 基于SRAM PUF的IIoT设备阈值认证方案，通过Hamming码纠错和时序多数投票将认证后误码率控制在1%以下，建立可靠性-安全性权衡的设计空间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为受限工业物联网设备提供硬件根植的认证方案，利用SRAM PUF的制造变异特性生成设备唯一响应，同时解决SRAM PUF固有的不可靠性问题

Method: 提出阈值认证概念验证，结合Hamming码纠错和时序多数投票的资源高效组合来稳定PUF响应；量化严格可靠性与安全性约束之间的阈值差距，将其重构为设计预算

Result: 方案能可靠地将认证后误码率控制在1%以下；增加Hamming码冗余或时序多数投票次数可显著降低误码率，但存在收益递减和计算开销增加的问题；较大响应长度使可靠性优化逐渐过时

Conclusion: 建立了PUF纠错的综合设计空间，指导未来实现中在纠错质量与计算需求、功耗、实现复杂度等资源约束之间取得平衡；设计预算方法允许资源感知地校准接受阈值、PUF响应长度和稳定技术

Abstract: Static Random Access Memory (SRAM) Physically Unclonable Functions (PUFs) make use of intrinsic manufacturing variations in memory cells to derive device-unique responses. Employing such hardware-rooted fingerprints for authentication, this work demonstrates a threshold-based authentication proof of concept for constrained Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) devices. The proposed scheme can reliably cap the the post-authentication bit error rate (BER) below 1 %. Inherent SRAM PUF unreliability is addressed by a resource-efficient combination of Hamming code (HC) Error Correction (EC) and Temporal Majority Voting (TMV). Increasing HC redundancy or TMV count significantly reduces the BER, albeit with diminishing returns and increasingly prohibitive computational overhead. Furthermore, this work quantifies the threshold gap between strict reliability and security constraints. This gap is reframed as a design budget which enables the resource-aware calibration of the acceptance threshold, PUF response length, and stabilization technique, without violating designed-for error limits. Larger responses make reliability optimizations increasingly obsolete. This comparative analysis establishes a comprehensive design space for PUF EC, guiding future implementations in balancing EC quality against resource constraints such as computational demand, power consumption, and implementation complexity.

</details>


### [100] [ProcRoute: Process-Scoped Authorization of Split-Tunnel Routes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16080)
*Arul Thileeban Sagayam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ProcRoute是一个系统，通过将路由访问建模为访问控制问题，限制内部路由仅对明确授权的应用程序可用，防止恶意进程滥用企业VPN/ZTNA中的内部路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大多数分割隧道VPN/ZTNA部署中，安装内部路由会授权整个设备而非特定应用程序使用该路由。这导致非特权恶意进程可以重用企业应用程序的路由来访问内部服务，存在安全风险。

Method: ProcRoute将路由访问建模为访问控制问题：应用程序身份作为主体，带端口和协议约束的目标前缀作为资源，默认拒绝的决策函数中介每个到内部目标的connect()和UDP sendmsg()调用。系统基于Linux cgroup v2和eBPF套接字地址钩子构建原型。

Result: 在双机WireGuard部署中，ProcRoute性能与WireGuard基线相当，比nftables cgroup匹配配置快13%，p50连接延迟为93μs（比基线增加3.6μs）。策略可扩展到5000个前缀，撤销时间低于毫秒。单机环回微基准测试显示低钩子开销：内部允许路径2.7μs，82/82次未授权尝试被阻止，策略重载期间120万次连接尝试中零次瞬时允许。

Conclusion: ProcRoute成功实现了细粒度的应用程序级路由访问控制，有效防止恶意进程滥用企业VPN内部路由，同时保持高性能和可扩展性，为分割隧道VPN/ZTNA部署提供了增强的安全解决方案。

Abstract: In most split-tunnel VPN/ZTNA deployments, installing an internal route authorizes the entire device, not a specific application, to use it. An unprivileged malicious process can therefore reach internal services by reusing routes intended for corporate applications. We present ProcRoute, a system that restricts internal-route access to explicitly authorized applications. ProcRoute models route access as an access-control problem: application identities are principals, destination prefixes with port and protocol constraints are resources, and a total, default-deny decision function mediates every connect() and UDP sendmsg() to an internal destination. Processes without a grant retain external access but are denied internal routes under our threat model. We describe ProcRoute's formal model, a Linux prototype built on cgroup v2 and eBPF socket-address hooks, and two complementary evaluations. In a two-machine WireGuard deployment, ProcRoute matches the WireGuard baseline and 13% faster than an nftables cgroup-matching configuration, with a p50 connect latency of 93 $μ$s (+3.6 $μ$s over baseline), flat policy scaling to 5,000 prefixes, and sub-millisecond revocation. Single-machine loopback microbenchmarks confirm low hook overhead: 2.7 $μ$s on the internal-allow path, 82/82 unauthorized pivot attempts blocked, and zero transient allows across 1.2 million connection attempts during policy reload.

</details>


### [101] [PolicyGapper: Automated Detection of Inconsistencies Between Google Play Data Safety Sections and Privacy Policies Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16128)
*Luca Ferrari,Billel Habbati,Meriem Guerar,Mariano Ceccato,Luca Verderame*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PolicyGapper：基于LLM的自动化工具，用于检测Google Play数据安全部分与隐私政策之间的不一致性，在330个热门应用中发现2689个遗漏披露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Google Play要求开发者提供数据安全部分披露数据实践，但缺乏自动化工具验证这些披露与隐私政策的一致性，且研究表明近80%热门应用存在不完整或误导性声明。

Method: PolicyGapper采用四阶段方法：1) 爬取应用数据安全部分和隐私政策；2) 预处理文本；3) 基于LLM分析一致性；4) 后处理结果。无需访问应用二进制文件。

Result: 在330个跨33个Google Play类别的热门应用数据集中，识别出2689个遗漏披露（2040个数据收集相关，649个数据共享相关）。手动验证显示平均精确率0.75，召回率0.77，准确率0.69，F1分数0.76。

Conclusion: PolicyGapper能有效自动检测数据安全部分与隐私政策之间的不一致性，为应用商店监管和开发者合规提供技术支持，并发布了完整复现包促进可重复性研究。

Abstract: Mobile application developers are required to disclose how they collect, use, and share user data in compliance with privacy regulations. To support transparency, major app marketplaces have introduced standardized disclosure mechanisms. In 2022, Google mandated the Data Safety Section (DSS) on Google Play, requiring developers to summarize their data practices. However, compiling accurate DSS disclosures is challenging, as they must remain consistent with the corresponding privacy policy (PP), and no automated tool currently verifies this alignment. Prior studies indicate that nearly 80% of popular apps contain incomplete or misleading DSS declarations. We present PolicyGapper, an LLM-based methodology for automatically detecting discrepancies between DSS disclosures and privacy policies. PolicyGapper operates in four stages: scraping, pre-processing, analysis, and post-processing, without requiring access to application binaries. We evaluate PolicyGapper on a dataset of 330 top-ranked apps spanning all 33 Google Play categories, collected in Q3 2025. The approach identifies 2,689 omitted disclosures, including 2,040 related to data collection and 649 to data sharing. Manual validation on a stratified 10% subset, repeated across three independent runs, yields an average Precision of 0.75, Recall of 0.77, Accuracy of 0.69, and F1-score of 0.76. To support reproducibility, we release a complete replication package, including the dataset, prompts, source code, and results available at https://github.com/Mobile-IoT-Security-Lab/PolicyGapper and https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19628493.

</details>


### [102] [TwoHamsters: Benchmarking Multi-Concept Compositional Unsafety in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15967)
*Chaoshuo Zhang,Yibo Liang,Mengke Tian,Chenhao Lin,Zhengyu Zhao,Le Yang,Chong Zhang,Yang Zhang,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了多概念组合不安全（MCCU）这一新漏洞，即单个良性概念通过组合产生不安全语义，并构建了包含17.5k提示的TwoHamsters基准来评估现有T2I模型和防御机制在此类风险上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像（T2I）模型的安全对齐主要关注显性恶意概念，忽视了组合语义中的微妙但关键的风险。为了解决这一盲点，作者识别并形式化了一个新的漏洞：多概念组合不安全（MCCU），即不安全语义源于个体良性概念的隐式关联。

Method: 1. 识别并形式化MCCU漏洞；2. 构建TwoHamsters基准，包含17.5k个专门设计用于探测MCCU漏洞的提示；3. 对10个最先进的T2I模型和16种防御机制进行严格评估；4. 通过分析得出8个关键见解。

Result: 评估显示当前T2I模型和防御机制面临严重的MCCU风险：在TwoHamsters基准上，FLUX模型的MCCU生成成功率高达99.52%，而LLaVA-Guard的召回率仅为41.06%。这突显了当前管理危险组合生成范式的关键局限性。

Conclusion: 当前T2I模型的安全对齐存在重大缺陷，特别是在处理组合语义风险方面。MCCU漏洞揭示了现有防御机制无法有效识别和阻止由良性概念组合产生的隐式不安全内容，需要新的范式来管理危险的组合生成。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable synthesis capabilities of text-to-image (T2I) models, safeguarding them against content violations remains a persistent challenge. Existing safety alignments primarily focus on explicit malicious concepts, often overlooking the subtle yet critical risks of compositional semantics. To address this oversight, we identify and formalize a novel vulnerability: Multi-Concept Compositional Unsafety (MCCU), where unsafe semantics stem from the implicit associations of individually benign concepts. Based on this formulation, we introduce TwoHamsters, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 17.5k prompts curated to probe MCCU vulnerabilities. Through a rigorous evaluation of 10 state-of-the-art models and 16 defense mechanisms, our analysis yields 8 pivotal insights. In particular, we demonstrate that current T2I models and defense mechanisms face severe MCCU risks: on TwoHamsters, FLUX achieves an MCCU generation success rate of 99.52%, while LLaVA-Guard only attains a recall of 41.06%, highlighting a critical limitation of the current paradigm for managing hazardous compositional generation.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [One-Shot Generative Flows: Existence and Obstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15439)
*Panos Tsimpos,Daniel Sharp,Youssef Marzouk*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究了具有独立端点的随机过程的动态测度传输，探讨了直线流（点加速度为零）的存在条件，揭示了高斯分布存在显式计算解而多峰分布不存在的二分现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究生成建模中动态测度传输的基本结构，特别关注端点独立的随机过程。动机在于理解何时存在直线流（straight-line flow）——这种流具有零加速度特性，可以被一阶方法精确积分，从而提供高效的生成模型。

Method: 1. 建立直线流的多种特征描述，通过涉及过程条件统计量的偏微分方程来刻画；2. 证明端点独立下直线流存在的尖锐二分性质；3. 构造高斯端点的显式计算直线过程；4. 通过一系列逐步推广的不可能性定理，证明具有充分分离模式的目标分布不存在直线流。

Result: 1. 建立了直线流的多重数学特征；2. 证明了直线流存在的二分现象：对于任意高斯端点可以构造显式计算解，但对于模式充分分离的目标分布不存在直线流；3. 揭示了独立端点过程的样本路径行为与其流映射的时空几何之间的基本关系。

Conclusion: 该研究为直线生成流何时存在提供了结构理论，揭示了高斯分布与多峰分布之间的根本差异，为生成建模中的动态测度传输方法提供了理论基础和限制条件。

Abstract: We study dynamic measure transport for generative modelling in the setting of a stochastic process $X_\bullet$ whose marginals interpolate between a source distribution $P_0$ and a target distribution $P_1$ while remaining independent, i.e., when $(X_0,X_1)\sim P_0\otimes P_1$.
  Conditional expectations of this process $X_\bullet$ define an ODE whose flow map transports from $P_0$ to $P_1$. We discuss when such a process induces a \emph{straight-line flow}, namely one whose pointwise acceleration vanishes and is therefore exactly integrable by any first-order method.
  We first develop multiple characterizations of straightness in terms of PDEs involving the conditional statistics of the process.
  Then, we prove that straightness under endpoint independence exhibits a sharp dichotomy.
  On one hand, we construct explicit, computable straight-line processes for arbitrary Gaussian endpoints. On the other hand, we show straight-line processes do not exist for targets with sufficiently well-separated modes. We demonstrate this through a sequence of increasingly general impossibility theorems that uncover a fundamental relationship between the sample-path behavior of a process with independent endpoints and the space-time geometry of this process' flow map. Taken together, these results provide a structural theory of when straight generative flows can, and cannot, exist.

</details>


### [104] [PRIM-cipal components analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15538)
*Tianhao Liu,Daniel Andrés Díaz-Pachón,J. Sunil Rao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在椭圆分布下存在两种同等最优但相反的凸点检测策略，分别通过选择最小或最大主成分来最大化或最小化方差，从而推导出无监督学习的无免费午餐定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督学习的无免费午餐定理已有深入研究，但无监督学习的NFLTs仍未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究在椭圆分布下是否存在普遍最优的凸点检测策略。

Method: 针对椭圆分布，从ℝ^d中剥离k个正交维度，每个维度保留概率为1-α的分位数区间。证明当选择k个最小主成分（最次要成分）时最大化总方差和Frobenius范数，当选择k个最大主成分时最小化它们。基于此开发了两种PRIM凸点检测算法：最小化方差法和最小化体积法。

Result: 在Fashion-MNIST数据集上的实验表明：剥离最大主成分能捕捉多样性（multiplicity），而剥离最小主成分能分离出流行风格（popular styles）。两种策略在科学意义上同等最优，但方向相反。

Conclusion: 存在两种同等最优的凸点检测策略，分别通过选择最小或最大主成分来优化不同目标，这为无监督学习提供了无免费午餐定理的理论基础。选择哪种策略取决于具体应用目标。

Abstract: Supervised No Free Lunch Theorems (NFLTs) are well studied, yet unsupervised NFLTs remain underexplored. For elliptical distributions, we prove that there exist two equally optimal, scientifically meaningful bump-hunting strategies that are exact opposites, with no universal winner. Specifically, peeling $k$ orthogonal dimensions from $\mathbb{R}^d$ ($d \ge k$), retaining an inter-quantile region of probability $1-α$ per peeled dimension, maximizes total variance and Frobenius norm when the $k$ smallest principal components (called pettiest components) are selected, and minimizes them when the selected dimensions are the $k$ leading principal components. These optima inspire PRIM-based bump-hunting algorithms either by minimizing variance or by minimizing volume, thereby motivating an NFLT. We test our results on the Fashion-MNIST database, showing that peeling the largest principal components captures multiplicity, while peeling the smallest principal components isolates popular styles.

</details>


### [105] [Adaptive multi-fidelity optimization with fast learning rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16239)
*Come Fiegel,Victor Gabillon,Michal Valko*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Kometo算法，用于多保真度优化问题，该算法无需已知函数平滑度和保真度假设，在简单遗憾方面达到接近理论下界的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多保真度优化中，存在不同成本和偏差的目标函数近似。研究者在有限预算下需要权衡近似成本与偏差，但现有方法通常需要已知问题相关参数（如函数平滑度、保真度假设）。

Method: 首先基于成本-偏差函数在不同保真度假设下证明了简单遗憾的下界。然后提出了Kometo算法，该算法无需已知函数平滑度和保真度假设，通过额外的对数因子达到相同收敛速率。

Result: Kometo算法在理论上改进了先前证明的保证，在经验上超越了不依赖问题参数知识的先前多保真度优化方法。

Conclusion: Kometo算法是一种自适应多保真度优化方法，无需先验知识即可达到接近最优性能，在实际应用中具有优势。

Abstract: In multi-fidelity optimization, biased approximations of varying costs of the target function are available. This paper studies the problem of optimizing a locally smooth function with a limited budget, where the learner has to make a tradeoff between the cost and the bias of these approximations. We first prove lower bounds for the simple regret under different assumptions on the fidelities, based on a cost-to-bias function. We then present the Kometo algorithm which achieves, with additional logarithmic factors, the same rates without any knowledge of the function smoothness and fidelity assumptions, and improves previously proven guarantees. We finally empirically show that our algorithm outperforms previous multi-fidelity optimization methods without the knowledge of problem-dependent parameters.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [Decompounding on Compact Symmetric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15980)
*Erik Kennerland*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了紧致对称空间上的随机解卷积问题（称为解复合问题），通过对称空间的调和分析构建了均方误差收敛的估计器，证明了收敛速率与欧几里得空间密度估计的渐近下界一致，并发现最优性取决于对称空间的秩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解复合问题在数学金融和波动光学等应用中广泛存在，但现有研究主要局限于紧致李群。将问题扩展到紧致对称空间可以覆盖统计学文献中常见的流形，同时需要解决观测步数未知的随机游走步分布估计问题。

Method: 利用对称空间的调和分析构造估计器，通过分析紧致对称空间上的一般密度估计问题，确定解复合问题属于一个子类，其下界取决于对称空间的秩。证明了估计器的收敛性并分析了收敛速率。

Result: 构建的估计器在均方误差意义下收敛，收敛速率与欧几里得空间密度估计的渐近下界一致。证明了紧致对称空间上一般密度估计问题具有相同速率，但解复合问题属于一个子类，其下界取决于对称空间的秩，因此估计器的最优性依赖于对称空间的秩。

Conclusion: 该研究将解复合问题扩展到紧致对称空间，建立了收敛的估计器并分析了其最优性。结果表明，与紧致李群上的类似问题相比，对称空间上的解复合问题具有改进的性能，且最优性条件取决于对称空间的秩，为相关应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper examines a stochastic deconvolution problem on compact symmetric spaces which is referred to as decompounding. This involves estimating the step distributions of a random walk, where in addition the number of steps between observations is unknown. The harmonic analysis of symmetric spaces is used to construct an estimator to the problem which converges in mean squared error, extending and improving on the analogous problem on compact Lie groups. The rates of convergence are shown to coincide with asymptotic lower bounds of density estimation in Euclidean space. We provide proofs that while the same rates hold for general density estimation problems in compact symmetric spaces, the decompounding problem lies in a subclass of these with different lower bounds depending on the rank of the space. Consequently, the optimality of the estimator depends on the rank of the symmetric space. Decompounding is a broad problem which appears in applications ranging from mathematical finance to wave optics, and the extension to compact symmetric spaces covers manifolds that commonly appear in the statistics literature.

</details>


### [107] [Simultaneous Inference for Covariance and Precision Matrices of Long-Range Dependent Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16219)
*Percy S. Zhai,Mladen Kolar,Wei Biao Wu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出针对长程依赖时间序列的协方差和精度矩阵推断方法，包括Berry-Esseen型高斯近似和块抽样bootstrap方法，适用于超高维设置


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于具有长程时间依赖性的时间序列，协方差矩阵和精度矩阵的推断具有挑战性。现有方法难以处理强时间依赖性和高维设置下的统计推断问题。

Method: 1. 提出Berry-Esseen型高斯近似结果，给出样本协方差矩阵估计误差无穷范数与相应高斯近似之间Kolmogorov距离的有限样本界；2. 利用鞅和m-依赖近似方法，构建三元块结构；3. 建立块抽样bootstrap方法，在强时间依赖性下保持有效性。

Result: 1. 协方差矩阵结果适用于超高维设置，时间序列维度可以随样本量呈次指数增长；2. 精度矩阵结果适用于低维设置；3. 方法不要求对协方差和精度矩阵的结构做任何假设。

Conclusion: 该方法为长程依赖时间序列的协方差和精度矩阵推断提供了有效的理论框架，特别适用于高维时间序列数据，具有较强的理论和实际应用价值。

Abstract: For time series with long-range temporal dependence, inference for covariance and precision matrices is non-trivial. We propose a Berry-Esseen type Gaussian approximation result that gives a finite-sample bound for the Kolmogorov distance between the infinity norms of the estimation error of sample covariance matrix and the corresponding Gaussian approximation. The method utilizes martingale and m-dependent approximation and relies on constructing triadic blocks. We also establish a bootstrapping result with block sampling method, which preserves validity despite strong temporal dependence. Our results on covariance allow ultra-high-dimensional settings where the dimension of time series can grow sub-exponentially with sample size. Similar results can be built for precision matrix under low-dimensional settings. No assumption is required on the structure of covariance and precision matrices.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [The Spectral Geometry of Thought: Phase Transitions, Instruction Reversal, Token-Level Dynamics, and Perfect Correctness Prediction in How Transformers Reason](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15350)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大语言模型在推理与事实回忆任务中，其隐藏激活空间表现出谱相变现象，揭示了思维几何的普适方向性、架构特异性动态及结果可预测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大语言模型在执行推理任务与事实回忆任务时，其内部隐藏激活空间的谱特性差异，以建立对Transformer推理过程的谱理论理解。

Method: 对11个模型（涵盖5种架构家族：Qwen、Pythia、Phi、Llama、DeepSeek-R1）进行系统谱分析，识别七个核心现象，包括谱压缩、指令微调反转、架构依赖生成分类、谱缩放定律、令牌级谱级联、推理步骤谱标点及谱正确性预测。

Result: 发现推理任务中9/11模型α值显著降低，指令微调模型呈现关系反转，建立了谱缩放定律（α_reasoning ∝ -0.074 ln N），令牌级同步随层距离指数衰减，推理步骤边界与相变特征对齐，谱α值在最终答案生成前即可高精度预测正确性（AUC达1.000）。

Conclusion: 研究建立了Transformer推理的全面谱理论，表明思维几何具有方向普适性、动态架构特异性及结果可预测性，为理解大语言模型的推理机制提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We discover that large language models exhibit \emph{spectral phase transitions} in their hidden activation spaces when engaging in reasoning versus factual recall. Through systematic spectral analysis across \textbf{11 models} spanning \textbf{5 architecture families} (Qwen, Pythia, Phi, Llama, DeepSeek-R1), we identify \textbf{seven} core phenomena: (1)~\textbf{Reasoning Spectral Compression} -- 9/11 models show significantly lower $α$ for reasoning ($p < 0.05$), with larger effects in stronger models; (2)~\textbf{Instruction Tuning Spectral Reversal} -- base models show reasoning $α< $ factual $α$, while instruction-tuned models reverse this relationship; (3)~\textbf{Architecture-Dependent Generation Taxonomy} -- prompt-to-response shifts partition into expansion, compression, and equilibrium regimes; (4)~\textbf{Spectral Scaling Law} -- $α_\text{reasoning} \propto -0.074 \ln N$ across 4 Qwen base models ($R^2 = 0.46$); (5)~\textbf{Token-Level Spectral Cascade} -- per-token alpha tracking reveals local synchronization that decays exponentially with layer distance, and is weaker for reasoning than factual tasks; (6)~\textbf{Reasoning Step Spectral Punctuation} -- phase-transition signatures align with reasoning step boundaries; and (7)~\textbf{Spectral Correctness Prediction} -- spectral $α$ alone achieves AUC $= 1.000$ (Qwen2.5-7B, late layers) and mean AUC $= 0.893$ across 6 models in predicting correctness \emph{before} the final answer is generated. Together, these findings establish a comprehensive \emph{spectral theory of reasoning} in transformers, revealing that the geometry of thought is universal in direction, architecture-specific in dynamics, and predictive of outcome.

</details>


### [109] [Sequential KV Cache Compression via Probabilistic Language Tries: Beyond the Per-Vector Shannon Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15356)
*Gregory Magarshak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出序列KV压缩方法，通过概率前缀去重和预测差分编码，利用语言模型的结构特性实现比现有方法高3个数量级的压缩比


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存量化方法（如TurboQuant）已达到每个向量压缩的香农熵极限，但这是针对较弱问题的解决方案。实际需要压缩的是作为序列的KV缓存，而KV缓存中的token来自模型训练的形式语言，模型本身是该语言的近乎最优预测器，可以利用这一结构特性实现更好的压缩。

Method: 提出两层架构的序列KV压缩：1）概率前缀去重：使用概率语言trie（PLT）的度量d_T(s, s') = -log_2 P_M(s ^ s')识别跨会话的语义等价共享前缀；2）预测差分编码：仅存储每个新KV向量与模型自身预测之间的残差，实现每个token的熵界H(KV_{i+1} | KV_{<=i}) <= H(token_{i+1} | token_{<=i})。

Result: 在典型语言模型困惑度（10-20）下，该方法平均每个token位置仅需3.3-4.3比特，而TurboQuant需要每个向量分量3比特（典型注意力头有64-128个分量）。理论压缩比相对于TurboQuant约为914,000倍。即使在熵底限1000倍以上的悲观最坏情况下（比实际源编码器典型2-5倍高两个数量级），压缩比仍保持约914倍，且随着上下文长度增加而改善。

Conclusion: 序列KV压缩通过利用语言模型的结构特性，实现了比现有方法高多个数量级的压缩比。该方法的两层架构正交且可与包括TurboQuant在内的现有每向量量化方法组合使用，压缩性能随上下文长度增长而改善而非退化。

Abstract: Recent work on KV cache quantization, culminating in TurboQuant, has approached the Shannon entropy limit for per-vector compression of transformer key-value caches. We observe that this limit applies to a strictly weaker problem than the one that actually matters: compressing the KV cache as a sequence. The tokens stored in a KV cache are not arbitrary floating-point data -- they are samples from the exact formal language the model was trained on, and the model is by construction a near-optimal predictor of that language. We introduce sequential KV compression, a two-layer architecture that exploits this structure. The first layer, probabilistic prefix deduplication, identifies semantically equivalent shared prefixes across sessions using the trie metric d_T(s, s') = -log_2 P_M(s ^ s') from Probabilistic Language Tries (PLTs). The second layer, predictive delta coding, stores only the residual of each new KV vector from the model's own prediction of it, achieving a per-token entropy bound of H(KV_{i+1} | KV_{<=i}) <= H(token_{i+1} | token_{<=i}). We prove that at typical language model perplexity -- approximately 10-20 for fluent English text -- this bound is 3.3-4.3 bits on average per token position, compared to TurboQuant's 3 bits per vector component (with typical attention heads having 64-128 components). The theoretical compression ratio over TurboQuant is approximately 914,000x at the Shannon limit. Even at 1000x above the entropy floor -- a deliberately pessimistic worst-case overhead, two orders of magnitude above the 2-5x typical of practical source coders -- the ratio remains approximately 914x over TurboQuant, with compression improving rather than degrading as context length grows. The two layers are orthogonal and compose with existing per-vector quantization methods including TurboQuant.

</details>


### [110] [Mapping High-Performance Regions in Battery Scheduling across Data Uncertainty, Battery Design, and Planning Horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15360)
*Jaime de Miguel Rodriguez,Artjom Vargunin,Brigitta Robin Raudne,David Solis Martin,Yaroslava Mykhailenko,Kaarel Oja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过三元分析探讨了多阶段模型预测控制下储能系统的运行，揭示了数据特征、预测不确定性、规划时域和电池倍率之间的相互作用，并确定了有效时域的概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解储能系统在多阶段模型预测控制下的运行特性，特别是数据特征、预测不确定性、规划时域和电池倍率之间的复杂相互作用，以优化储能运行策略并降低计算成本。

Method: 采用三元分析方法，生成合成数据集来系统探索数据剖面和不确定性的变化，通过参数化建立这些特征与最优时域长度之间的映射关系，研究不同电池类型、不确定性水平和数据剖面的组合。

Result: 研究发现存在"有效时域"的概念，即超过该时域长度后，额外的预测信息对运行性能提升有限；考虑有效时域可降低计算成本同时保持最优性能；量化了预测不确定性导致的收益损失，显示即使对快速电池也有影响；提供了各种组合下的最优时域长度实用指南。

Conclusion: 该研究为工业储能运行提供了实用指导，建立了数据集参数化与最优时域之间的映射框架，为未来机器学习方法支持工业环境中的连续优化而不需要大量计算奠定了基础。

Abstract: This study presents a triadic analysis of energy storage operation under multi-stage model predictive control, investigating the interplay between data characteristics, forecast uncertainty, planning horizon, and battery c-rate. Synthetic datasets are generated to systematically explore variations in data profiles and uncertainty, enabling parametrization and the construction of relationships that map these characteristics to optimal horizon length. Results reveal the presence of an effective horizon, defined as the look-ahead length beyond which additional forecast information provides limited operational benefit. Accounting for this horizon can reduce computational costs while maintaining optimal performance. The study provides optimal horizon lengths across a broad range of combinations of battery types, uncertainty levels, and data profiles, offering practical guidance for industrial storage operation. It also quantifies revenue losses due to forecast uncertainty, showing that errors can impact performance even for fast batteries. Finally, the framework lays the groundwork for future machine learning approaches that map dataset parametrization to optimal horizons, supporting continuous optimization in industrial settings without heavy computation.

</details>


### [111] [M3R: Localized Rainfall Nowcasting with Meteorology-Informed MultiModal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15377)
*Sanjeev Panta,Rhett M Morvant,Xu Yuan,Li Chen,Nian-Feng Tzeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: M3R是一种基于多模态注意力机制的气象感知架构，通过融合NEXRAD雷达图像和个人气象站测量数据进行直接降雨预测，在准确性和效率方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的降雨临近预报对灾害缓解和水资源管理至关重要。当前深度学习方法在有效利用多样化多媒体数据源方面存在局限，需要开发能够协同利用多种气象数据的新型预测架构。

Method: 提出M3R架构，通过专门的多模态注意力机制，将气象站时间序列作为查询来选择性关注空间雷达特征，实现降水特征的聚焦提取。采用综合管道实现异构气象数据的时间对齐，协同结合视觉NEXRAD雷达图像和数值个人气象站测量数据。

Result: 在三个以NEXRAD雷达站为中心的100km×100km区域进行实验，M3R在准确性、效率和降水检测能力方面均优于现有方法，取得了显著改进。

Conclusion: M3R为基于多媒体的降水临近预报建立了新的基准，为业务天气预报系统提供了实用工具。该工作展示了多模态数据融合在气象预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate and timely rainfall nowcasting is crucial for disaster mitigation and water resource management. Despite recent advances in deep learning, precipitation prediction remains challenging due to limitations in effectively leveraging diverse multimedia data sources. We introduce M3R, a Meteorology-informed MultiModal attention-based architecture for direct Rainfall prediction that synergistically combines visual NEXRAD radar imagery with numerical Personal Weather Station (PWS) measurements, using a comprehensive pipeline for temporal alignment of heterogeneous meteorological data. With specialized multimodal attention mechanisms, M3R novelly leverages weather station time series as queries to selectively attend to spatial radar features, enabling focused extraction of precipitation signatures. Experimental results for three spatial areas of 100 km * 100 km centered at NEXRAD radar stations demonstrate that M3R outperforms existing approaches, achieving substantial improvements in accuracy, efficiency, and precipitation detection capabilities. Our work establishes new benchmarks for multimedia-based precipitation nowcasting and provides practical tools for operational weather prediction systems. The source code is available at https://github.com/Sanjeev97/M3Rain

</details>


### [112] [Hallucination as Trajectory Commitment: Causal Evidence for Asymmetric Attractor Dynamics in Transformer Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15400)
*G. Aytug Akarlar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现自回归语言模型中的幻觉是由早期轨迹承诺和不对称吸引子动力学控制的，幻觉轨迹在第一步生成时就与事实轨迹分离，纠正幻觉需要多步协调干预，而诱导幻觉只需单次扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自回归语言模型中幻觉现象的根本机制，理解为什么模型会生成与事实不符的内容，以及这种幻觉是如何在生成过程中形成和维持的。

Method: 使用相同提示分叉方法，通过重复采样相同输入观察自发分叉；采用激活修补技术分析因果不对称性；通过窗口修补研究干预需求；使用残差状态分析和无监督聚类识别提示编码阶段的机制。

Result: 44.3%的提示在第一生成token处就出现事实与幻觉轨迹分叉；幻觉激活注入正确轨迹导致87.5%输出损坏，而反向干预仅恢复33.3%；纠正需要多步持续干预，而损坏只需单次扰动；步骤0残差状态可预测幻觉率（r=0.776）；识别出5个类似机制群组，其中鞍点邻近群组集中了12/13的分叉错误前提提示。
